**[00:51:52.780] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000511)  
Mr. President, Ms. President, I move that we take up the budget of the Department of Transportation. I so move, Mr. President.

**[00:51:58.700] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000511-split-2)  
Any objection? Hearing none, motion is approved.

**[00:52:01.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000512)  
We recognize Senator J.V. Ejercito to give the highlights of the budget.

**[00:52:06.300] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000512-split-2)  
We recognize Senator J.V. Ejercito to sponsor the budget of the Department of Transportation.

**[00:52:14.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000512-split-3)  
Mr. President, this representation is honored to sponsor the budget of the Department of Transportation, its attached agencies and corporations. For the record, the recommended new appropriations for the Department of Transportation and its attached agencies is

**[00:52:28.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000513)  
Php 294,568,744,000 plus Php 1,228,338,000 for its attached corporations. With a total budget of Php 295,797,082,000, we are pleased to present some of the key highlights of the Senate Committee Report.

**[00:53:01.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000514)  
We have restored the funding for the two vital foreign-assisted infrastructure projects to their respective national expenditure program levels. For the Metro Manila Subway project, we restored 19.7 billion covering both the loan proceeds and the government counterpart. Meanwhile, for the North-South Commuter Railway project, we restored 15 billion in loan proceeds. These restorations are crucial to ensuring the timely implementation and completion of these transformative infrastructure projects

**[00:53:32.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000515)  
honoring our commitments to international development partners and preventing the government from incurring additional commitment fees and other unnecessary financial costs arising from project delays. We have likewise increased the budget for the Active Transport Bike Share System and Safe Pathways Program and the NCR busway to provide our commuting public with safer, more accessible, efficient, and sustainable transportation options.

**[00:54:02.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000516)  
Lastly, we have provided additional funding for the improvement and upgrading of various airports across the country to enhance regional connectivity, [unclear], and economic development and ensure safer, more convenient, and reliable air travel for our Kababayans. Mr. President, we are now ready to answer questions from our colleagues.

**[00:54:24.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000517)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[00:58:20.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000633)

**[00:58:31.780] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000639)

**[00:58:33.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000640)

**[00:58:41.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000644)

**[00:58:43.840] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000645)

**[00:58:45.920] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000646)

**[00:58:50.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000648)

**[00:58:54.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000650)

**[00:58:55.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000651)

**[00:58:57.700] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000652)

**[00:59:08.720] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000658)

**[00:59:14.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000660)

**[00:59:17.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000662)

**[00:59:33.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000670)  
So yun lamang po, segfin sa what is

**[01:00:00.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-000684)  
namin

Philippines.

**[01:00:39.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000704)  
but unfortunately the beautiful

**[01:00:42.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000705)

**[01:00:44.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000706)

**[01:00:46.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000707)

**[01:00:48.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000708)

**[01:00:49.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000709)  
turboprop

**[01:00:51.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000710)

**[01:00:53.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000711)  
at the moment. And the reason, the very reason

**[01:00:56.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000712)  
why turboprop

**[01:00:57.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000713)

**[01:00:59.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000714)  
not allowed anymore to operate

**[01:01:01.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000715)

**[01:01:03.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000716)

**[01:01:05.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000717)  
Mr. President, because

**[01:01:06.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000718)  
yung sequencing ng landing

**[01:01:09.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000719)

**[01:01:11.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000720)  
you only have

**[01:01:13.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000721)

**[01:01:15.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000722)

**[01:01:18.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000723)

**[01:01:19.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000724)

**[01:01:21.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000725)  
sequencing of

**[01:01:22.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000726)

**[01:01:26.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000727)  
Yung pong mga jets

**[01:01:27.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000728)  
have the same takeoff and

**[01:01:29.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000729)

**[01:01:31.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000730)  
yung turboprop, it disrupts everything.

**[01:01:34.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000731)  
So that is probably

**[01:01:35.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000732)  
years ago we had a problem

**[01:01:37.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000733)

**[01:01:40.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000734)

**[01:01:42.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000735)  
Mr. President, ito yung time na

**[01:01:43.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000736)

**[01:01:46.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000737)

**[01:01:48.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000738)

**[01:01:50.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000739)  
So that was the immediate

**[01:01:52.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000740)

**[01:01:54.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000741)  
had to take out the

**[01:01:56.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000742)

**[01:01:58.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000743)

**[01:01:59.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000744)

**[01:02:01.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000745)

the

**[01:02:04.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000747)

**[01:02:07.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000748)

**[01:02:09.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000749)

**[01:02:11.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000750)

**[01:02:12.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000751)

**[01:02:15.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000752)

**[01:02:17.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000753)

**[01:02:19.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000754)

**[01:02:21.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000755)  
kasi ang isang turboprop, magugulo

**[01:02:23.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000756)  
huy talaga yun. So that

**[01:02:25.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000757)

**[01:02:27.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000758)

**[01:02:29.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000759)

**[01:02:31.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000760)

**[01:02:33.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000761)

**[01:02:35.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000762)

**[01:02:37.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000763)

**[01:02:42.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000766)

**[01:02:44.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000767)

**[01:02:46.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000768)

**[01:02:48.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000769)

**[01:02:50.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000770)

**[01:02:54.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000772)

**[01:03:31.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000783)

**[01:03:36.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000785)

**[01:03:38.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000786)  
we have, as the one who has

**[01:03:50.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000792)  
Kasama na po dun yung paglente ng mga

So, the long-term plan is really to

**[01:23:39.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001435)

**[01:23:40.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001436)

**[01:23:44.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001438)

**[01:23:46.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001439)

**[01:23:50.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001441)

**[01:23:52.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001442)

**[01:23:54.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001443)

**[01:23:56.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001444)

**[01:23:57.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001445)

**[01:24:00.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001446)

**[01:24:02.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001447)

**[01:24:04.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001448)

**[01:24:06.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001449)

**[01:24:09.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001450)

**[01:24:10.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001451)

**[01:24:12.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001452)

**[01:24:13.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001453)

**[01:24:15.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001454)  
to lengthen the runways so that

**[01:24:18.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001455)

**[01:24:20.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001456)

**[01:24:22.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001457)  
jet-engined aircraft, Your Honor,

**[01:24:24.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001458)  
Mr. President.

**[01:24:28.860] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001460)

**[01:24:48.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001470)  
or taking

**[01:24:50.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001471)

for these like Roro,

**[01:25:06.560] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001479)

**[01:25:08.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001480)  
Marinduque, for example,

**[01:25:10.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001481)  
the connectivity of these

**[01:25:12.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001482)

**[01:25:13.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001483)

**[01:25:15.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001484)

**[01:25:18.200] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001485)  
Romblon, they want to go to Marinduque.

**[01:25:20.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001486)  
And then they want to go to El Nido.

**[01:25:23.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001487)  
Pupwede naman ang

**[01:25:24.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001488)

**[01:25:26.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001489)

**[01:25:28.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001490)

**[01:25:30.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001491)

**[01:25:32.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001492)

**[01:25:34.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001493)

**[01:25:36.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001494)  
Wala po tayong magandang pier,

**[01:25:38.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001495)  
Sen, kundi

**[01:25:40.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001496)

**[01:25:42.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001497)

**[01:25:44.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001498)

**[01:25:46.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001499)  
they have wonderful beaches there,

**[01:25:48.680] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001500)  
Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:25:50.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001501)  
Mr. President, in Marinduque,

**[01:25:53.040] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001502)  
in Romblon,

**[01:25:54.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001503)  
hindi lang po sa El Nido.

**[01:25:56.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001504)  
So meron po silang ferry, meron po

**[01:25:58.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001505)

**[01:26:00.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001506)  
The problem we have now is

**[01:26:02.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001507)

**[01:26:03.580] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001508)

**[01:26:05.840] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001509)

**[01:26:10.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001510)  
Your Honor, the PPA

**[01:26:11.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001511)

**[01:26:13.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001512)  
Atty. Santiago, and according to him,

**[01:26:16.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001513)  
most of the ports are already

**[01:26:18.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001514)  
capable of accepting rural

**[01:26:20.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001515)

**[01:26:22.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001516)  
Ang problema daw ho,

**[01:26:24.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001517)

**[01:26:26.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001518)

**[01:26:28.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001519)

**[01:26:30.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001520)

**[01:26:31.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001521)

**[01:26:34.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001522)

**[01:26:36.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001523)  
Mindoro, and

**[01:26:37.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001524)  
sa Palawan po, they have rural ports

**[01:26:40.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001525)  
that they have ports

**[01:26:42.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001526)  
that can accommodate rural vessels.

**[01:26:44.580] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001527)  
Tama po yun. The problem

**[01:26:46.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001528)  
daw is yung pong

**[01:26:48.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001529)  
parang terminal na mga

**[01:26:50.200] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001530)  
pasahero. They have to wait.

**[01:26:52.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001531)  
Yung po yun na pa sila.

**[01:26:54.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001532)  
Kasi umikot po tayo

**[01:26:55.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001533)  
in these provinces.

**[01:26:58.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001534)  
Yung pong problema, yung pag-wait

**[01:27:00.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001535)  
po nila, wala.

**[01:27:02.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001536)  
Tsaka napansin ko rin,

**[01:27:04.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001537)  
Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary, Mr. President,

**[01:27:07.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001538)  
wala pong nakalang

**[01:27:08.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001539)

**[01:27:09.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001540)  
Ports Authority. May we know the reason?

**[01:27:12.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001541)  
Kasi hindi lang po

**[01:27:14.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001542)  
mga

**[01:27:16.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001543)  
these provinces. May mga

**[01:27:17.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001544)

**[01:27:20.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001545)  
Palawan na wala po silang

**[01:27:21.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001546)

**[01:27:24.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001547)  
Kailangan po nilang

**[01:27:25.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001548)

**[01:27:28.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001549)

**[01:27:30.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001550)

**[01:27:32.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001551)  
sa amin, yung mga malalaking pump boat.

**[01:27:34.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001552)  
Hindi ho. Kasi wala hong maayos

**[01:27:36.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001553)  
na pier.

**[01:27:38.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001554)  
Yun ho yung problem ngayon.

**[01:27:40.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001555)  
May plano po ba ang PPA?

**[01:27:42.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001556)  
Ilan po ang plano ng PPA

**[01:27:44.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001557)

**[01:27:46.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001558)  
ng mga pier? Kung hindi man po

**[01:27:48.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001559)  
para sa mga roro natin,

**[01:27:50.200] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001560)  
para sa mga fishing boats

**[01:27:52.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001561)  
po natin.

**[01:27:53.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001562)  
Mr. President, when it comes to fishing,

**[01:27:55.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001563)

**[01:27:57.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001564)  
DA ang may in-charge po sa mga

**[01:27:59.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001565)

**[01:28:01.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001566)  
Just to go back to your

**[01:28:03.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001567)  
earlier concern,

**[01:28:06.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001568)

**[01:28:07.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001569)

**[01:28:09.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001570)

**[01:28:11.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001571)

**[01:28:12.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001572)

**[01:28:16.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001573)

**[01:28:17.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001574)

**[01:28:18.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001575)  
PPA,

**[01:28:20.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001576)

**[01:28:24.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001578)

**[01:28:26.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001579)

**[01:28:28.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001580)  
the passenger terminals.

**[01:28:31.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001581)  
Kung meron man,

**[01:28:32.560] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001582)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[01:28:34.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001583)  
ang problem po na nakita po natin,

**[01:28:37.280] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001584)

**[01:28:38.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001585)  
Kung meron po magdarating na barko sa isang

**[01:28:40.560] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001586)

**[01:28:42.820] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001587)

**[01:28:44.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001588)

**[01:28:46.580] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001589)  
Because I was there, I saw it myself.

**[01:28:49.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001590)  
Anyway, I'm from there.

**[01:28:51.560] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001591)  
Moving on to my

**[01:28:54.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001593)

**[01:29:07.120] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001599)

**[01:29:10.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001600)  
Mr. Chairman?

**[01:29:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001601)  
You're correct, Mr. President.

**[01:29:16.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001602)  
From the HGAP, zero po yan.

**[01:29:18.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001603)  
But your Senate Committee added

**[01:29:20.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001604)

**[01:29:22.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001605)

**[01:29:24.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001606)

**[01:29:26.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001607)  
Naglagay po tayo ng 150 million.

**[01:29:28.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001608)  
Alright. Thank you very much, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:29:30.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001609)  
Well, Mr. President,

**[01:29:31.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001610)  
this representation supports

**[01:29:33.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001611)

**[01:29:36.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001612)  
of the Department of Transportation.

**[01:29:39.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001613)  
Maraming salamat po and good afternoon.

Senator Erwin.

So, he requested that we requested him, if possible, to allow the budget to be deemed submitted without prejudice to him asking about the budget once again or the Secretary setting an appointment and then he can discuss these issues privately. **[02:37:00.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002672)  
the go-between is Senator Joel Villanueva. He's our... He will represent me because I cannot be out of the floor, Mr. President.

**[02:37:07.180] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002672-split-2)

**[02:37:12.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002672-split-3)  
Yes.

**[02:37:12.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002672-split-4)

**[02:37:17.980] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002672-split-5)

**[02:37:22.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002672-split-6)

**[02:37:27.200] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002672-split-7)

**[02:37:30.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002673)

**[02:38:00.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002674)

**[02:56:39.100] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-002766)

**[02:56:47.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002768)  
Maraming salamat po, Ginong Pangulo.

**[02:56:50.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002769)  
Mr. Chairman, matanong ko lamang po.

**[02:56:54.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002770)  
Kasi po, nung isang araw, during the budget hearing of the Department of Tourism,

**[02:57:00.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002771)  
napag-usapan po, na-raise po ng isang kasamahan natin patungkol po sa ilang aksidente sa ating mga tourist destinations.

**[02:57:08.580] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002772)  
Ang gusto nyo pang itanong, and I believe,

**[02:57:11.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002773)  
doon sa mga areas na may mga beaches, may nalunod po yata doon sa paglangoy sa isang ilog,

**[02:57:22.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002774)  
walang nakalagay na sign.

**[02:57:25.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002775)  
Ang tanong ho niya, at tatanong din natin,

**[02:57:28.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002776)

**[02:57:30.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002777)

**[02:57:36.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002778)

**[02:57:41.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002779)

**[02:57:47.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002780)  
Meron pong naka-organized na tourist police.

**[02:57:51.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002781)  
Naka-deploy po ito sa walong major tourist destinations sa buong bansa.

**[02:57:56.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002782)  
So, kumpleto po sila ng gamit, may patrol cars, may motorsiklo, lahat po, full complement po yan.

**[02:58:04.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002783)  
But my question, Mr. Chair, is what about doon sa security po?

**[02:58:10.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002784)  
Opo.

**[02:58:11.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002785)

**[02:58:17.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002786)  
ang ating mga tourist police po?

**[02:58:19.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002787)  
Hindi lang po sa mga malalaking areas, kundi sa mga bayan po na may mga falls,

**[02:58:27.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002788)

**[02:58:31.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002789)

**[02:58:33.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002790)

**[02:58:41.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002791)  
Mr. Chair?

**[02:58:43.420] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002792)  
Kumpleto po yung training nila.

**[02:58:45.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002793)  
Katunayan po para sa susunod na budget year,

**[02:58:49.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002794)  
nag-allocate po tayo ng additional 176.899 million pesos para lalo sila maging episyente po sa kanilang tungkulin.

**[02:59:01.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002795)

**[02:59:04.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002796)

**[02:59:05.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002797)  
Meron na silang talagang pondo para sa kanilang duties.

**[02:59:11.180] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002798)  
Responsibilities.

**[02:59:11.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002799)  
Nagdagdag pa po tayo ng 176 million.

**[02:59:14.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002800)

**[02:59:16.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002801)

**[02:59:25.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002803)

**[02:59:29.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002804)

**[02:59:33.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002805)

**[02:59:35.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002806)

**[02:59:40.660] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002807)

**[02:59:44.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002808)

**[02:59:47.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-002809)

**[03:04:03.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002907)

**[03:04:09.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002910)

**[03:04:10.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002911)

**[03:04:15.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002913)

**[03:04:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002914)

**[03:04:19.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002915)

million for its 9-1-1

**[03:04:25.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002918)

**[03:04:30.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002921)

**[03:04:33.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002922)

**[03:04:35.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002923)

**[03:04:36.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002924)

**[03:04:39.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002925)

**[03:04:45.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002928)

**[03:04:49.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002930)

**[03:04:52.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002931)  
The Philippine Public Safety

**[03:04:57.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002934)

This would strengthen police operations,

**[03:16:15.040] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-003057-split-2)  
Hearing none, consideration of the budget of the Department of Housing?

**[03:16:23.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003057-split-3)  
Yes. Is it in order? May we recognize the sponsor,

**[03:16:29.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003058)  
Senator JV Ejercito, to present the highlights of the budget.

**[03:16:33.180] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-003058-split-2)  
Senator JV Ejercito, Chairman of the Committee on Finance, is recognized to sponsor the budget.

**[03:16:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003058-split-3)  
Thank you, Mr. President. This representation is honored to sponsor the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development and its attached agencies and corporations. For the record, the recommended new appropriations of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development and its attached agencies and corporations is P6,401,681,000.

**[03:17:02.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003059)  
In the Senate Committee Report, we provided additional funding for the Socialized Housing Loan Takeout Receivable for the Shelter Program and the Housing Project for Military Retirees on a Philippine Veterans Affairs Office property in Taguig City. We have also allocated an additional 400 million for various housing projects under the National Housing Authority and 504.8 million for the Community Mortgage Program under the Social Housing Finance Corporation.

**[03:17:32.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003060)  
These additional allocations underscore our commitment to addressing the country's housing backlog, expanding access to affordable and decent housing. Mr. President, we are now ready to answer the questions from our colleagues.

**[03:17:46.980] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-003060-split-2)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:17:48.780] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003060-split-3)  
Mr. President, if I approve the budget today, it will be faster than all the budgets prior to previous to them, including the Office of the President. But there seems to be no one who wishes to interpellate the budget

**[03:18:00.300] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003061)

**[03:18:29.500] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003062)

**[03:18:42.380] Senator Vicente C. Sotto III** (asr-003062-split-2)

**[04:09:23.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003219)

**[04:09:36.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003225)

**[04:09:41.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003227)

**[04:09:43.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003228)

**[04:09:58.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003235)

**[04:10:07.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003239)

**[04:10:21.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003246)

**[04:10:39.200] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003254)

**[04:10:40.160] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003255)

**[04:10:50.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003259)

**[04:10:51.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003260)

**[04:10:53.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003261)

**[04:10:56.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003262)

**[04:10:58.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003263)  
sa ilalim ng DENR.

**[04:11:00.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003264)

**[04:11:06.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003266)

**[04:11:15.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003270)

**[04:11:16.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003271)

Mr. President,

**[04:11:41.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003283)

**[04:11:43.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003284)

**[04:11:46.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003285)

**[04:11:51.860] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003288)

**[04:11:54.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-003289)

**[05:42:52.600] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003611)  
Thank you very much, Mr. President. Mr. President, I'll call out the budgets of two agencies left. And then whoever would like to ask questions on the agencies are

**[05:42:59.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003614)  
most welcome.

**[05:43:01.820] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003615)  
We'd like to call out or take in consideration the budget of the Games and Amusements Board

**[05:43:06.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003616)  
as well as the Philippine Sports Commission

**[05:43:09.540] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003617)  
and ask the good sponsor, Senator Christopher Bongo, to sponsor both measures.

**[05:43:17.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003618)  
Give the highlights of the budgets of the two measures.

**[05:43:20.320] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003619)  
We recognize the sponsor for the Games and Amusements Board

**[05:43:25.020] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003620)  
as well as the Philippine Sports Commission, Senator Bongo.

**[05:43:28.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003621)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[05:43:30.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003622)  
For fiscal year 2027 for the Philippine Sports Commission,

**[05:43:35.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003623)  
in our committee report, the total new appropriations is 1,891,444,000 pesos.

**[05:43:47.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003624)  
The increase of 580 million is in our committee report

**[05:43:50.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003625)  
is mostly due to the additional funding for hosting of international competitions,

**[05:43:56.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003626)  
construction of regional sports,

**[05:43:58.340] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003627)  
football centers, grassroots sports programs, and assistance to athletes.

**[05:44:04.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003628)  
And for the Games and Amusements Board, in our committee report,

**[05:44:08.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-003629)  
is 157,230,000 pesos, no increase.

**[05:44:16.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003630)  
Thank you. Thank you, Your Honor.

**[05:44:19.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003631)  
Mr. President, we'd like to ask, we have two colleagues here.

**[05:44:21.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003632)  
I think they want to propound some questions or manifestations to the agencies.

**[05:44:25.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003633)  
Should it be ladies first?

**[05:44:28.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003634)  
Oh, yes, ladies first.

**[05:44:30.060] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003635)  
Senator Pia Cayetano.

**[05:44:31.340] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003636)  
We recognize Senator Pia Cayetano.

**[05:44:39.240] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003637)  
Before we recognize Senator Cayetano, Mr. President,

**[05:44:41.860] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003638)  
we'd just like to put on record,

**[05:44:44.160] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003639)  
the Philippine Sports Commission Chair, Pato Gregorio,

**[05:44:47.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003640)  
had asked to be excused because he's in FIFA today

**[05:44:50.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003641)  
trying to get a $25 million sports development, football development initiative

**[05:44:56.260] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003642)  
to build a multipurpose...

**[05:44:58.340] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003643)  
...football stadium and...

**[05:45:00.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003644)  
as other initiatives.

**[05:45:02.140] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003645)  
I said that it's okay as long as he comes back next week on Thursday

**[05:45:05.620] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003646)  
before the budget is approved and ratified on second reading.

**[05:45:11.560] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003647)  
So he'll be coming, Mr. President, as told.

**[05:45:13.880] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003648)  
He'll be here Thursday morning.

**[05:45:15.060] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003649)  
In fact, I had the opportunity to meet the FIFA president during ASEAN.

**[05:45:20.000] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003650)  
I think he was here.

**[05:45:22.460] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003651)  
Pato Gregorio introduced me to them.

**[05:45:24.140] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003652)  
And yes, FIFA is very interested in investing in the Philippines

**[05:45:27.960] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003653)  
to boost our sports activities here.

**[05:45:33.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003654)  
Mr. President, if I may.

**[05:45:35.480] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003655)  
Yes, we recognize Senator Joe.

**[05:45:37.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003656)  
This is just a short manifestation with regard to the manifestation

**[05:45:41.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003657)  
or statements made by our distinguished majority leader.

**[05:45:45.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003658)  
I just want to put on record, Mr. President,

**[05:45:47.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003659)  
that since the day I became a member of the legislature,

**[05:45:53.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003660)  
not too long ago, Mr. President,

**[05:45:55.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003661)  
in the House of Representatives and as a member of this August Chamber

**[05:46:01.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003662)  
of this institution, we have yet to deliberate on a budget

**[05:46:06.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003663)  
without the head or chair of the agency or the department.

**[05:46:12.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003664)  
However, Mr. President, we are aware of the importance of this meeting

**[05:46:19.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003665)  
being held right now outside the country.

**[05:46:21.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003666)  
And we feel that it is important that,

**[05:46:25.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003667)  
as the head of FIFA who went here in the Philippines,

**[05:46:30.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003668)  
go out of his way to come visit our beloved country.

**[05:46:34.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003669)  
And so I was told, Mr. President, that the meeting being held outside the country right now

**[05:46:43.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003670)  
is a very important meeting where we can actually get an opportunity

**[05:46:49.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003671)  
to have a lot of grants and

**[05:46:52.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003672)  
the

**[05:46:55.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003673)  
details with the FIFA headed, of course, by our Philippine Sports Commission chairperson,

**[05:47:03.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003674)  
Pato Gregorio.

**[05:47:05.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003675)  
We post no objection, Mr. President.

**[05:47:08.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003676)  
However, we'd like to put on record that this should not be a precedent

**[05:47:13.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003677)  
and this should not be emulated by anyone heading the departments or agencies

**[05:47:20.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003678)  
because today, Mr. President, no less than the vice president herself,

**[05:47:25.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003679)  
came here and showed up during the budget deliberation.

**[05:47:31.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003680)  
So that's all, Mr. President, for us to be clear.

**[05:47:35.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003681)  
Thank you very much, Mr. President.

**[05:47:36.340] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003682)  
Yes, it's a time-honored tradition that the head of agency comes to the Senate

**[05:47:41.900] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003683)  
to defend their own budget, to justify their own budget.

**[05:47:45.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003684)  
That's their budget, so they should be the ones to defend it.

**[05:47:49.640] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003685)  
So this is an exception rather than a rule.

**[05:47:53.520] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003686)  
And that's why, too,

**[05:47:55.000] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003687)  
to compensate that exception before the budget gets approved on second reading,

**[05:48:01.040] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003688)  
they will be requested to come here to the Senate

**[05:48:04.000] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003689)  
to participate in the discussions.

**[05:48:10.080] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003690)  
Yes, and Mr. President, that's why we're requesting them to come

**[05:48:12.900] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003691)  
before the period of amendments

**[05:48:14.520] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003692)  
because a failure for them to come

**[05:48:17.000] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003693)  
will also, I suggest, that we just move their budget elsewhere

**[05:48:20.760] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003694)  
or we just lessen their budget

**[05:48:22.400] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003695)  
and give it to another...

**[05:48:24.640] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003696)  
department who are more willing to defend their budget.

**[05:48:29.320] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003697)  
So their promise naman is to come before the period of amendments.

**[05:48:33.480] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003698)  
So, forewarned, those agencies that were not here,

**[05:48:38.360] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003699)  
the heads of agencies were not here,

**[05:48:40.180] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003700)  
we're forewarning them now

**[05:48:41.580] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003701)  
that there's a chance na mabawasan po yung budget nila

**[05:48:44.740] Senator Juan Miguel "Migz" F. Zubiri** (asr-003702)  
kung di sila pong sumipot dito.

**[05:48:46.460] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003703)  
Correct.

**[05:48:46.840] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003704)  
And like what Senator Joel mentioned,

**[05:48:49.480] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003705)  
no less than the vice president of our country came here

**[05:48:52.580] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003706)  
to defend her budget and to...

**[05:48:54.640] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003707)  
justify her budget for 2027.

**[05:48:58.920] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003708)  
So we'll make sure

**[05:49:00.940] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003709)  
well, the people from the PSC is here

**[05:49:05.420] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003710)  
so I'm sure that they will relay the message to Mr. Gregorio.

**[05:49:10.540] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003711)  
So with that, we recognize Senator Pia Cayetano.

**[05:49:14.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003712)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[05:49:16.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003713)  
Good evening to everyone,

**[05:49:18.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003714)  
Chairman and the entire PSC and GAB family.

**[05:49:23.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003715)  
I don't...

**[05:49:24.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003716)  
really have any questions.

**[05:49:26.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003717)  
I will just be manifesting my support and my concerns

**[05:49:29.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003718)  
for a few areas.

**[05:49:30.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003719)  
And then if there's anyone who wants to relay

**[05:49:34.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003720)  
to the chairman a quick response, it's okay.

**[05:49:37.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003721)  
But basically, it's really my manifestation of support.

**[05:49:42.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003722)  
So with the permission of the chair, I will proceed.

**[05:49:45.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003723)  
I want to start with grassroots programs.

**[05:49:49.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003724)  
When Filipinos watch our elite athletes succeed,

**[05:49:54.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003725)  
and obviously, that comes with so much pride and joy,

**[05:49:58.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003726)  
it's true that many don't know the challenges

**[05:50:03.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003727)  
that these athletes went through to get to that elite level.

**[05:50:07.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003728)  
But with social media today,

**[05:50:09.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003729)  
and even AI that comes up with all these interesting

**[05:50:13.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003730)  
mini-documentaries,

**[05:50:16.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003731)  
nagkakaroon na sila ng picture

**[05:50:17.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003732)  
of how these athletes got to where they are.

**[05:50:22.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003733)  
And on the good side is they realize

**[05:50:24.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003734)  
the Filipinos realize that there's no shortcut.

**[05:50:27.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003735)  
Wala namang nakarating ng ganyang level na

**[05:50:29.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003736)  
hindi nagbuhos ng pagod, hirap, gumastos.

**[05:50:36.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003737)  
But at the same time,

**[05:50:38.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003738)  
it tends to highlight

**[05:50:40.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003739)  
areas that we can still work on.

**[05:50:44.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003740)  
And of course, that revolves around funding.

**[05:50:47.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003741)  
Kasi a lot of these talents can be...

**[05:50:50.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003742)  
There are a lot of talents in the provinces.

**[05:50:54.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003743)  
And the opportunities there are really less.

**[05:50:57.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003744)  
So, I speak from experience

**[05:50:59.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003745)

**[05:51:26.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003755)

**[05:51:28.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003756)

**[05:51:32.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003757)

**[05:51:34.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003758)

**[05:51:37.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003759)

**[05:51:43.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003761)

**[05:51:48.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003762)

**[05:51:52.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003763)

**[05:51:53.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003764)

I would say half-half.

**[05:51:57.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003766)  
Metro Manila and then all over the country na.

**[05:52:01.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003767)

**[05:52:03.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003768)  
So, meaning may magaganda na rin

**[05:52:05.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003769)

**[05:52:19.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003775)

**[05:52:25.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003778)

Negros.

Pupunta ng Manila.

Okay?

**[05:52:58.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003795)  
I guess, Mr. Chairman, Mr. President,

**[05:53:03.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003797)

**[05:53:11.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003799)

**[05:53:13.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003800)

**[05:53:16.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003801)  
Imposible naman kasi yung all parts right away.

**[05:53:19.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003802)  
Imposible.

**[05:53:19.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003803)  
I know because I'm supporting the development

**[05:53:22.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003804)

**[05:53:24.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003805)  
So, it just so happens na I was asking my staff,

**[05:53:29.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003806)

**[05:53:31.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003807)

**[05:53:43.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003810)

**[05:53:46.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003812)

**[05:53:54.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003816)

**[05:53:55.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003817)

Medyo mas malayo si pickleball,

**[05:54:06.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003822)

**[05:54:08.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003823)  
But for paddle and hybrid racing,

**[05:54:11.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003824)

**[05:54:14.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003825)

**[05:54:18.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003826)  
Anyway, my point is,

**[05:54:19.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003827)

**[05:54:21.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003828)

**[05:54:40.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003836)  
So, yun lang naman.

**[05:55:18.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003854)

**[05:55:20.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003855)

**[05:55:20.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003856)

**[05:55:22.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003857)  
Sige, sige.

**[05:55:23.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003858)  
May kita ka dyan,

**[05:55:24.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003859)

**[05:55:24.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003860)

**[05:55:25.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003861)

**[05:55:26.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003862)  
It's a chicken and an egg.

**[05:55:27.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-003863)

the Gab has tasked with investigating alleged game-fixing and other irregularities in Philippine sports under its jurisdiction.

**[06:32:51.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004815)

**[06:32:57.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004816)

**[06:32:59.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004817)  
Mga law enforcement agencies.

**[06:33:01.880] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004818)  
Anyway, about the other question a while ago.

**[06:33:05.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004819)

**[06:33:13.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004820)

**[06:33:15.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004821)

**[06:33:22.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004822)

**[06:33:29.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004823)  
Perhaps a mechanism that you can develop to look into this.

**[06:33:38.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004824)

**[06:33:45.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004825)

**[06:33:54.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004826)  
Meron sila nun.

**[06:33:54.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004827)

**[06:33:59.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004828)

**[06:34:12.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004829)  
So that it would be easier when to alert everyone, the public, the league itself.

**[06:34:21.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004830)

**[06:34:29.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004831)

**[06:34:36.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004832)

**[06:34:43.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004833)  
So I hope maka-develop po tayong ganyan.

**[06:34:45.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004834)  
And I will go now to another issue, hindi lang sa another agency, which is the Philippine Sports Commission.

**[06:34:57.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004835)  
Kasi kayo yung dyan naman.

**[06:34:59.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004836)

**[06:35:03.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004837)

**[06:35:05.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004838)

**[06:35:08.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004839)

**[06:35:11.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004840)

**[06:35:12.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004841)

**[06:35:13.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004842)

**[06:35:14.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004843)

**[06:35:21.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004844)

**[06:35:22.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004845)

**[06:35:23.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004846)  
Nagsisiyan, nagsusuntukan.

**[06:35:25.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004847)  
Nanalo po sila.

**[06:35:26.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004848)

**[06:35:29.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-004849)

**[06:36:24.020] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004863)

**[06:36:39.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004868)

**[06:36:59.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004873)

**[06:37:07.060] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004875)

**[06:37:12.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004876)

**[06:37:19.860] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004877)

**[06:37:22.280] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004878)

**[06:37:25.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004879)

**[06:37:27.980] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004880)

**[06:37:29.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004881)

**[06:37:31.180] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004882)

**[06:37:31.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004883)  
Nawawala.

**[06:37:33.080] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004884)  
So anyway, I'm urging you.

**[06:37:35.520] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004885)

**[06:37:38.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-004886)
